\---

\# 1. Purpose

G1 menetapkan constitutional foundation bagi seluruh hubungan integrasi PD-03 dengan Platform lain dalam AIOS.

G1 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana PD-03 berinteraksi dengan Platform lain tanpa mengaburkan ownership, authority, accountability, dependency, atau boundary masing-masing Platform?\*\*

G1 menjadi parent constitutional layer bagi:

\- Platform interfaces;

\- cross-platform dependencies;

\- governance integration;

\- compliance integration;

\- certification integration;

\- shared governance services;

\- integration contracts;

\- integration review and control;

\- platform integration success.

\---

\# 2. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization mengidentifikasi Part G sebagai:

\> \*\*Platform Integration\*\*

dengan concern:

\- Platform Interfaces;

\- Cross-Platform Dependencies;

\- Shared Services;

\- Integration Contracts. Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Concern tersebut menjadi source-supported foundation bagi G1.

Namun source yang tersedia tidak memberikan definisi literal:

\> \*\*G1 — Platform Integration Constitution\*\*

secara eksplisit.

Oleh karena itu, G1 menggunakan bounded canonical synthesis untuk membentuk constitutional parent dari concern Part G tersebut.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Integration Principle

Platform integration shall enable governed interaction between domains while preserving the authority, accountability, ownership, and boundary of each participating Platform.

Integration harus memungkinkan:

• coordination;

• dependency management;

• evidence exchange;

• governance interaction;

• compliance interaction;

• certification interaction;

• shared services;

• contractual exchange.

Integration tidak boleh secara otomatis menciptakan:

• ownership transfer;

• organizational subordination;

• authority transfer;

• execution takeover.

⸻

5\. Platform Ownership Principle

PD-03 memiliki primary domain:

Governance & Compliance

Baseline menetapkan PD-03 sebagai Governance Authority dengan primary responsibility pada:

• Policy;

• Control;

• Certification.

Technical Design tidak dimiliki PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Karena itu:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

↓

Governance Integration

↓

Other Platform Domain

↓

Domain Owner

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner atas domain Platform lain hanya karena governance relationship diperlukan.

⸻

6\. Integration Does Not Transfer Ownership

Canonical rule:

Integration does not transfer domain ownership.

Relationship:

Integration

≠

Ownership Transfer

Jika PD-03 membutuhkan evidence dari Platform lain:

PD-03

Governance Requirement

↓

Applicable Platform

↓

Domain Owner

↓

Evidence / Action

↓

PD-03

Assessment

Domain owner tetap bertanggung jawab terhadap execution dalam domainnya.

⸻

7\. Dependency Does Not Mean Subordination

Canonical rule:

Dependency does not establish organizational subordination.

Contoh:

PD-03

│

├── depends on Architecture

├── depends on Security

└── depends on Quality

Dependency tersebut tidak menjadikan Architecture, Security, atau Quality sebagai subordinate organizational unit PD-03.

Source secara eksplisit menetapkan Architecture, Security, dan Quality sebagai dependencies PD-03, bukan subordinate units.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

8\. Governance Does Not Mean Execution Ownership

PD-03 dapat menetapkan governance requirements terhadap Platform lain.

Namun:

Governance Requirement

↓

Applicable Platform

↓

Domain Execution

Execution tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

Source secara eksplisit menetapkan:

Governance responsibility does not automatically transfer domain ownership.

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap Governance Control.

Namun governance control tidak berarti PD-03 menjalankan seluruh operational control implementation.

Governance Control

↓

PD-03

Control Implementation

↓

Applicable Domain Owner

Source secara eksplisit membedakan governance control dari operational control execution.

Certification ≠ Implementation Ownership

13\. Out of Scope

G1 tidak mengambil ownership terhadap:

• technical implementation;

• runtime execution;

• infrastructure operation;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

• architecture ownership;

• domain capability execution;

• organizational restructuring;

• workforce management;

• operational management Platform lain;

• enterprise strategic direction.

PD-03 dapat memiliki interface dengan domain tersebut apabila diperlukan untuk governance.

Interface tidak mengubah ownership.

⸻

14\. Platform Interface Principle

Platform interface adalah mekanisme hubungan antara dua atau lebih domain.

Interface dapat membawa:

• requirements;

• policy;

• standards;

• decisions;

• evidence;

• findings;

• certification information;

• compliance status;

• remediation status;

• lifecycle information.

Interface bukan organizational hierarchy.

Canonical rule:

Interface

≠

Hierarchy

⸻

15\. Cross-Platform Dependency Principle

Setiap material dependency harus dapat diidentifikasi.

Minimum conceptual relationship:

Provider

↓

Dependency

↓

Consumer

Dependency harus memiliki:

• purpose;

• applicable boundary;

• owner;

• expected input/output;

• evidence requirement apabila applicable;

• failure handling apabila material.

Catatan: field-level dependency schema tersebut adalah bounded synthesis; source belum memberikan canonical schema G1-specific.

⸻

16\. Dependency Ownership

Provider dan consumer tidak kehilangan ownership hanya karena dependency.

Provider

owns

↓

Provided Domain / Capability

Consumer

owns

↓

Consumed Domain Outcome

Integration contract hanya mengatur relationship.

Contract tidak menciptakan organizational ownership baru.

⸻

17\. Shared Services Principle

Part G source mengidentifikasi Shared Services sebagai concern integration.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Untuk PD-03, shared services dapat mencakup governance-related services apabila memang canonical dalam baseline.

Potential examples:

• governance policy interface;

• compliance assessment;

• certification coordination;

• governance evidence exchange;

• governance reporting.

Status: examples di atas merupakan bounded synthesis dan tidak boleh dianggap sebagai canonical service catalogue sampai source reconciliation selesai.

18\. Shared Service Boundary

Shared service harus memiliki:

• service owner;

• consumer;

• purpose;

• interface;

• boundary;

• responsibility;

• service expectation;

• evidence apabila applicable.

Shared service tidak boleh menciptakan implicit authority atas consumer.

⸻

Governance integration memungkinkan PD-03 menerapkan governance requirement kepada applicable Platform.

↓

Applicability

↓

Domain Interpretation

↓

↓

Evidence

↓

Governance Assessment

PD-03 menetapkan governance requirement.

Domain owner menjalankan domain execution.

Compliance integration memungkinkan:

• requirement exchange;

• assessment;

• findings;

• remediation;

Requirement

↓

Applicability

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Finding

↓

Remediation

21\. Certification Integration Principle

Certification integration harus mempertahankan separation:

Certification Authority

↓

Certification Assessment

↓

↓

22\. Integration Contract Principle

Part G source secara eksplisit mengidentifikasi Integration Contracts sebagai salah satu concern utama.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Integration contract harus digunakan untuk membuat relationship explicit.

Contract dapat mendefinisikan:

• parties;

• purpose;

• interface;

• responsibility;

• inputs;

• outputs;

• evidence;

• timing;

• failure handling;

• escalation;

• change mechanism.

Detail mandatory contract schema tetap membutuhkan source reconciliation.

⸻

23\. Contract Does Not Create Authority

Canonical rule:

An integration contract defines a relationship; it does not create authority beyond the authority already granted by the applicable architecture.

Authority

↓

Permitted Relationship

↓

Integration Contract

Bukan:

Integration Contract

↓

New Authority

⸻

24\. Cross-Platform Accountability

Setiap material integration relationship harus memiliki accountability yang jelas.

Minimum distinction:

Authority

≠

Ownership

≠

Accountability

≠

Execution

Integration harus menghindari situasi di mana:

• provider tidak accountable;

• consumer tidak accountable;

• responsibility overlap;

• failure ownership tidak jelas.

⸻

25\. Cross-Platform Evidence

Material integration harus menghasilkan evidence yang dapat ditelusuri.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• interface record;

• dependency record;

• contract;

• decision;

• approval;

• evidence exchange;

• compliance result;

• certification result;

• review result;

• exception;

• remediation.

Evidence harus tetap berada dalam canonical record system yang applicable.

⸻

26\. Integration Traceability

Material integration relationship harus dapat ditelusuri:

Requirement

↓

Interface

↓

Dependency

↓

Contract

↓

Action

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Outcome

Traceability membantu memastikan bahwa integration tidak menjadi informal relationship yang tidak dapat diaudit.

⸻

27\. Integration Decision Principle

Jika integration relationship membutuhkan decision:

1\. identifikasi matter;

2\. identifikasi affected Platforms;

3\. identifikasi applicable authority;

4\. assess impact;

5\. obtain required decision;

6\. record decision;

7\. implement relationship;

8\. verify result.

Integration tidak boleh menciptakan informal authority melalui operational habit.

⸻

28\. Integration Change Principle

Perubahan material terhadap integration relationship harus melalui applicable change mechanism.

Contoh:

Change Proposal

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Affected Platform Review

↓

Authority Decision

↓

Contract / Interface Update

↓

Implementation

↓

Verification

Boundary change harus explicit.

Source PD-03 menegaskan bahwa boundary tidak berubah melalui kebiasaan operasional.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

29\. Cross-Platform Conflict

Jika integration conflict terjadi:

Identify Conflict

↓

Classify Domain

↓

Identify Applicable Authority

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve Within Domain if Possible

↓

Escalate if Required

↓

Record Outcome

Conflict tidak boleh diselesaikan hanya berdasarkan organizational preference.

Applicable authority dan domain boundary menjadi basis.

⸻

30\. Integration Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila:

• ownership ambiguity;

• material dependency failure;

• unresolved contract conflict;

• authority conflict;

• cross-platform impact;

• material compliance issue;

• material lifecycle consequence.

Escalation mechanism harus mengikuti applicable Governance Architecture.

G1 tidak menciptakan escalation authority baru.

⸻

31\. Platform Autonomy

Setiap Platform tetap mempertahankan autonomy dalam domainnya.

PD-02 → Architecture

PD-03 → Governance & Compliance

PD-04 → Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 → Runtime & Execution

PD-06 → AI Engineering

PD-07 → Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 → Security

PD-09 → Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 → Developer Experience

G1 tidak mengubah domain ownership tersebut.

⸻

32\. Cross-Platform Governance Rule

Canonical rule:

Governance alignment does not equal centralized execution control.

PD-03 dapat memastikan:

• requirement alignment;

• compliance;

• governance evidence;

• certification;

• accountability.

Namun execution tetap berada pada domain owner.

⸻

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance requirement yang memiliki architectural implication.

Namun:

Governance Requirement

≠

Automatic Architecture Change

Architecture change tetap harus mengikuti applicable Architecture Governance mechanism.

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan separation antara Governance dan Architecture.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

34\. Performance Relationship

Performance evidence dapat menjadi input bagi integration review.

Namun:

Performance

≠

Integration Ownership

Performance Architecture tetap memiliki boundary sendiri.

G1 hanya menetapkan bahwa integration relationship dapat menggunakan applicable performance evidence apabila diperlukan.

36\. Integration Review Principle

Material integration harus dapat direview.

Review dapat menilai:

• interface validity;

• dependency health;

• contract compliance;

• evidence quality;

• ownership integrity;

• authority integrity;

• cross-platform impact;

• unresolved findings.

G1 hanya menetapkan constitutional requirement.

Detailed review mechanism diturunkan ke section integration review yang relevan.

⸻

37\. Integration Control Principle

Integration control harus menjaga:

• boundary;

• accountability;

• evidence;

• contract;

• dependency;

• compliance;

• continuity.

Control tidak boleh menjadi mechanism untuk mengambil alih execution.

⸻

38\. Integration Success Principle

Integration success tidak berarti:

semua Platform harus terhubung.

Integration success berarti relationship:

• legitimate;

• purposeful;

• owned;

• traceable;

• governed;

• operationally usable;

• boundary-preserving.

G10 akan mendefinisikan outcome layer secara lebih rinci.

⸻

39\. Integration Failure

Integration failure dapat berupa:

• interface failure;

• dependency failure;

• contract failure;

• evidence failure;

• authority failure;

• ownership ambiguity;

• unresolved cross-platform conflict;

• material boundary violation.

Failure harus menjadi input bagi applicable:

• review;

• control;

• lifecycle;

• improvement.

⸻

40\. Integration Integrity

Integration integrity terdiri atas:

Authority Integrity

-

Ownership Integrity

-

Boundary Integrity

-

Contract Integrity

-

Evidence Integrity

-

Traceability

-

Continuity

Jika salah satu dimensi material mengalami failure, integration outcome harus direview.

⸻

41\. No Implicit Integration

Tidak ada relationship yang menjadi canonical hanya karena:

• sering digunakan;

• sudah menjadi kebiasaan;

• dianggap understood;

• terjadi secara informal.

Material integration harus memiliki explicit architectural/governance basis.

⸻

42\. No Implicit Authority

Integration activity tidak boleh menghasilkan authority yang tidak tercatat.

Existing Authority

↓

Permitted Integration

Bukan:

Existing Integration

↓

Assumed Authority

⸻

43\. No Implicit Ownership

Tidak boleh terjadi:

PD-03 provides governance

↓

PD-03 owns domain

atau:

PD-03 certifies Platform

↓

PD-03 owns implementation

atau:

PD-03 depends on Platform

↓

Platform becomes subordinate

Ketiga pola tersebut bertentangan dengan ownership principles PD-03.

⸻

44\. Constitutional Rules

GIC-01 — Integration Without Ownership Transfer

Integration tidak memindahkan ownership.

GIC-02 — Dependency Without Subordination

Dependency tidak menciptakan hierarchy.

GIC-03 — Governance Without Execution Takeover

Governance requirement tidak memindahkan execution ownership.

GIC-04 — Certification Without Implementation Ownership

Certification tidak memberikan implementation ownership.

GIC-05 — Contract Without New Authority

Integration contract tidak menciptakan authority baru.

GIC-06 — Explicit Boundary

Material integration boundary harus explicit.

GIC-07 — Explicit Accountability

Material integration harus memiliki accountability.

GIC-08 — Evidence-Based Integration

Material integration harus dapat dibuktikan.

GIC-09 — Traceable Integration

Material integration harus traceable.

GIC-10 — Platform Autonomy

Setiap Platform mempertahankan domain ownership.

46\. Out-of-Scope Enforcement

Jika suatu proposal integration ternyata membutuhkan:

• new organizational ownership;

• new governance authority;

• architecture ownership;

• technical execution;

• operational ownership;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

proposal tersebut harus dikembalikan kepada applicable architecture/domain owner.

G1 tidak boleh digunakan sebagai shortcut untuk memperluas authority PD-03.

Karena baseline literal G1 belum ditemukan, Claude Code WAJIB melakukan source reconciliation sebelum G1 dianggap fully canonical.

Claude Code harus memeriksa:

1\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

2\. Platform Organization Specification;

5\. Platform Interface definitions;

• integration authority;

• integration ownership;

Jika source tidak ditemukan, jangan mengarang historical baseline.

Gap harus dicatat sebagai:

Architecture Gap / Claude Code Completion Required

49\. Success Criteria

G1 dianggap structurally successful apabila:

1\. Part G memiliki constitutional foundation;

2\. Platform interface memiliki boundary;

3\. dependency memiliki ownership distinction;

4\. shared service tidak menciptakan implicit authority;

5\. integration contract tidak menciptakan authority baru;

6\. governance integration tidak mengambil alih execution;

7\. compliance integration tidak mengambil alih domain ownership;

8\. certification tidak mengambil alih implementation;

9\. cross-platform accountability dapat ditentukan;

10\. material integration dapat ditelusuri;

11\. material integration memiliki evidence;

12\. integration change memiliki applicable change mechanism;

13\. conflict memiliki escalation path;

14\. Platform autonomy tetap terjaga;

15\. Architecture ownership tetap berada pada applicable domain;

16\. technical execution tetap berada pada applicable domain;

17\. G1 tidak mengambil alih G2–G10;

18\. G1 tidak mengulang substantive content Parts A–F;

19\. source gaps diberi qualification;

20\. Claude Code dapat merekonsiliasi setiap bounded synthesis terhadap canonical corpus.

51\. Canonical Constitutional Statement

G1 — Platform Integration Constitution establishes the constitutional boundary for PD-03 interaction with other AIOS Platforms. Integration shall enable governed interfaces, dependency management, shared governance relationships, compliance and certification interaction, and explicit integration contracts while preserving the authority, accountability, ownership, autonomy, and domain boundary of every participating Platform.

Governance interaction does not transfer execution ownership. Dependency does not establish subordination. Certification does not establish implementation ownership. Integration contracts do not create authority beyond the authority already granted by the applicable architecture.

Material cross-platform relationships shall be explicit, appropriately authorized, accountable, evidence-based, and traceable.

⸻

\# 1. Purpose

G2 mendefinisikan architectural boundary untuk interface antara PD-03 Governance & Compliance dengan Platform lain dalam AIOS.

G2 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana PD-03 berinteraksi dengan Platform lain melalui interface yang jelas tanpa mengubah ownership, authority, accountability, atau execution boundary masing-masing Platform?\*\*

G2 merupakan child architecture dari:

\> \*\*G1 — Platform Integration Constitution\*\*

G1 menetapkan constitutional principles.

G2 menerjemahkan prinsip tersebut ke dalam architecture interface.

\# 2. Source Foundation

Platform Organization baseline mengidentifikasi Part G sebagai:

\> \*\*Platform Integration\*\*

dengan concern:

\- Platform Interfaces;

\- Cross-Platform Dependencies;

\- Shared Services;

\- Integration Contracts. Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian, \*\*Platform Interfaces\*\* merupakan source-supported concern untuk Part G.

Baseline PD-03 juga menetapkan bahwa PD-03 dapat memiliki organizational interface dengan Platform lain, termasuk:

\- PD-02 Architecture;

\- PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence;

\- PD-05 Runtime & Execution;

\- PD-06 AI Engineering;

\- PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform;

\- PD-08 Security;

\- PD-09 Quality & Evaluation;

\- PD-10 Developer Experience. Performance Architecture Review.txt

Relationship tersebut secara eksplisit mencakup:

\- governance dependency;

\- compliance interface;

\- evidence exchange;

\- policy applicability;

\- control relationship;

\- certification relationship. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\# 3. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline yang tersedia \*\*tidak memberikan definisi literal bernama G2 — Platform Interface Architecture\*\*.

Baseline juga belum memberikan canonical:

\- interface taxonomy;

\- interface contract schema;

\- mandatory interface fields;

\- interface lifecycle schema;

\- interface SLA;

\- interface protocol;

\- interface registry format.

Karena itu G2 menggunakan:

Source-Supported Interface Concerns

-

G1 Constitutional Boundary

-

Existing PD-03 Interface Evidence

↓

Bounded Canonical Synthesis

Tidak ada field atau protocol teknis yang boleh dianggap sebagai historical canonical baseline tanpa rekonsiliasi Claude Code.

5\. Fundamental Interface Principle

A Platform Interface is an explicit relationship mechanism between domains; it does not create organizational hierarchy or transfer domain ownership.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

│ Interface

▼

Applicable Platform

│

│ Domain Execution

▼

Domain Outcome

Interface menghubungkan domain.

Interface tidak menggabungkan ownership domain.

⸻

6\. Interface Does Not Create Ownership

Canonical rule:

Interface

≠

Ownership Transfer

Jika PD-03 berinteraksi dengan PD-08 Security:

PD-03

Governance Requirement / Compliance

│

▼

PD-08

Security Domain

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner Security.

Sebaliknya, PD-08 tidak menjadi owner Governance & Compliance.

Setiap Platform mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

7\. Interface Does Not Create Hierarchy

Interface tidak berarti:

• reporting relationship;

• organizational subordination;

• staffing authority;

• command hierarchy;

• execution authority.

Canonical rule:

Interface ≠ Organizational Hierarchy

Prinsip ini merupakan konsekuensi langsung dari boundary PD-03 bahwa governance relationship tidak memindahkan ownership Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

19\. Interface Purpose

Setiap material interface harus memiliki legitimate purpose.

Potential source-supported purposes untuk PD-03 meliputi:

• governance dependency;

• compliance interface;

• evidence exchange;

• policy applicability;

• control relationship;

• certification relationship.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Interface tidak boleh dibuat hanya untuk:

• memperluas authority;

• menciptakan reporting line;

• mengambil alih domain;

• menggantikan existing owner.

⸻

20\. Interface Categories

Berdasarkan source yang tersedia, PD-03 interface dapat diklasifikasikan secara bounded sebagai:

20.1 Governance Interface

Pertukaran governance requirement atau governance context.

20.2 Compliance Interface

Hubungan untuk applicability, assessment, evidence, finding, atau verification.

20.3 Evidence Interface

Pertukaran evidence yang diperlukan untuk governance/compliance.

20.4 Policy Applicability Interface

Hubungan untuk menentukan applicability governance policy terhadap domain tertentu.

20.5 Control Interface

Hubungan antara governance control responsibility dan domain implementation/evidence.

20.6 Certification Interface

Hubungan certification antara PD-03 dan subject Platform.

Qualification: taxonomy ini adalah canonical synthesis dari source-supported relationship types, bukan taxonomy verbatim baseline.

⸻

↓

Interface

\=

Architectural Relationship

Explicit Agreement / Constraint

for the Relationship

sementara G8 akan menjawab "apa kontrak relationship tersebut?"

Karena source literal G2 belum ditemukan, Claude Code WAJIB melakukan reconciliation terhadap canonical corpus sebelum G2 dianggap fully canonical.

Claude Code harus mencari:

0\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

0\. Platform Organization Specification;

0\. Volume 0 Platform Organization Master Map;

0\. Architecture Baseline;

0\. PD-02 interface definitions;

0\. PD-03 A1–A10;

0\. PD-03 F1–F10;

0\. existing Interface Registry;

0\. existing Interface Contracts;

0\. Platform Dependency Map;

0\. cross-platform ADR;

0\. canonical integration artifacts.

Claude Code harus mencari apakah source memiliki:

• canonical interface names;

• interface owners;

• interface consumers;

• interface providers;

• interface direction;

• interface purpose;

• interface lifecycle;

• interface contract;

• interface evidence;

• interface registry;

• interface authority;

• interface success criteria.

Jika ditemukan, source tersebut menggantikan bounded synthesis pada bagian terkait.

Jika tidak ditemukan:

Do not invent historical baseline.

Catat gap sebagai:

Architecture Gap — Claude Code Completion Required

57\. Success Criteria

G2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. seluruh material PD-03 cross-platform relationships memiliki interface boundary;

0\. interface purpose dapat dijelaskan;

0\. interface tidak menciptakan ownership transfer;

0\. interface tidak menciptakan hierarchy;

0\. interface tidak menciptakan authority baru;

0\. domain ownership masing-masing Platform tetap jelas;

0\. governance interface dapat dibedakan dari execution;

0\. material interface dapat menghasilkan evidence;

0\. interface change dapat mengikuti applicable lifecycle;

0\. interface conflict memiliki escalation path;

0\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G3;

0\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G8;

0\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G9;

0\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G10;

0\. seluruh bounded synthesis dapat direkonsiliasi oleh Claude Code.

59\. Canonical Interface Principle

G2 — Platform Interface Architecture defines the explicit cross-platform interface relationships through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance interacts with other AIOS Platform Divisions. Interfaces shall preserve domain ownership, authority, accountability, and execution boundaries; support legitimate governance, compliance, evidence, policy applicability, control, and certification relationships; and shall not create organizational hierarchy or authority beyond the authority already established by the applicable architecture.

\---

\# 1. Purpose

G2 mendefinisikan architectural boundary untuk interface antara PD-03 Governance & Compliance dengan Platform lain dalam AIOS.

G2 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana PD-03 berinteraksi dengan Platform lain melalui interface yang jelas tanpa mengubah ownership, authority, accountability, atau execution boundary masing-masing Platform?\*\*

G2 merupakan child architecture dari:

\> \*\*G1 — Platform Integration Constitution\*\*

G1 menetapkan constitutional principles.

G2 menerjemahkan prinsip tersebut ke dalam architecture interface.

\---

\# 2. Source Foundation

Platform Organization baseline mengidentifikasi Part G sebagai:

\> \*\*Platform Integration\*\*

dengan concern:

\- Platform Interfaces;

\- Cross-Platform Dependencies;

\- Shared Services;

\- Integration Contracts. Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian, \*\*Platform Interfaces\*\* merupakan source-supported concern untuk Part G.

Baseline PD-03 juga menetapkan bahwa PD-03 dapat memiliki organizational interface dengan Platform lain, termasuk:

\- PD-02 Architecture;

\- PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence;

\- PD-05 Runtime & Execution;

\- PD-06 AI Engineering;

\- PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform;

\- PD-08 Security;

\- PD-09 Quality & Evaluation;

\- PD-10 Developer Experience. Performance Architecture Review.txt

Relationship tersebut secara eksplisit mencakup:

\- governance dependency;

\- compliance interface;

\- evidence exchange;

\- policy applicability;

\- control relationship;

\- certification relationship. Performance Architecture Review.txt

\---

\# 3. Source-Fidelity Qualification

Baseline yang tersedia \*\*tidak memberikan definisi literal bernama G2 — Platform Interface Architecture\*\*.

Baseline juga belum memberikan canonical:

\- interface taxonomy;

\- interface contract schema;

\- mandatory interface fields;

\- interface lifecycle schema;

\- interface SLA;

\- interface protocol;

\- interface registry format.

Karena itu G2 menggunakan:

Source-Supported Interface Concerns

-

G1 Constitutional Boundary

-

Existing PD-03 Interface Evidence

↓

Bounded Canonical Synthesis

Tidak ada field atau protocol teknis yang boleh dianggap sebagai historical canonical baseline tanpa rekonsiliasi Claude Code.

5\. Fundamental Interface Principle

A Platform Interface is an explicit relationship mechanism between domains; it does not create organizational hierarchy or transfer domain ownership.

Canonical relationship:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

│ Interface

▼

Applicable Platform

│

│ Domain Execution

▼

Domain Outcome

Interface menghubungkan domain.

Interface tidak menggabungkan ownership domain.

⸻

6\. Interface Does Not Create Ownership

Canonical rule:

Interface

≠

Ownership Transfer

Jika PD-03 berinteraksi dengan PD-08 Security:

PD-03

Governance Requirement / Compliance

│

▼

PD-08

Security Domain

PD-03 tidak menjadi owner Security.

Sebaliknya, PD-08 tidak menjadi owner Governance & Compliance.

Setiap Platform mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

7\. Interface Does Not Create Hierarchy

Interface tidak berarti:

• reporting relationship;

• organizational subordination;

• staffing authority;

• command hierarchy;

• execution authority.

Canonical rule:

Interface ≠ Organizational Hierarchy

Prinsip ini merupakan konsekuensi langsung dari boundary PD-03 bahwa governance relationship tidak memindahkan ownership Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Provider

│ Interface

Consumer

Namun:

Dependency

≠

Subordination

19\. Interface Purpose

Setiap material interface harus memiliki legitimate purpose.

Potential source-supported purposes untuk PD-03 meliputi:

• governance dependency;

• compliance interface;

• evidence exchange;

• policy applicability;

• control relationship;

• certification relationship.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Interface tidak boleh dibuat hanya untuk:

• memperluas authority;

• menciptakan reporting line;

• mengambil alih domain;

• menggantikan existing owner.

⸻

20\. Interface Categories

Berdasarkan source yang tersedia, PD-03 interface dapat diklasifikasikan secara bounded sebagai:

20.1 Governance Interface

Pertukaran governance requirement atau governance context.

20.2 Compliance Interface

Hubungan untuk applicability, assessment, evidence, finding, atau verification.

20.3 Evidence Interface

Pertukaran evidence yang diperlukan untuk governance/compliance.

20.4 Policy Applicability Interface

Hubungan untuk menentukan applicability governance policy terhadap domain tertentu.

20.5 Control Interface

Hubungan antara governance control responsibility dan domain implementation/evidence.

20.6 Certification Interface

Hubungan certification antara PD-03 dan subject Platform.

Qualification: taxonomy ini adalah canonical synthesis dari source-supported relationship types, bukan taxonomy verbatim baseline.

⸻

Interface

\=

Architectural Relationship

Explicit Agreement / Constraint

for the Relationship

sementara G8 akan menjawab "apa kontrak relationship tersebut?"

56\. Claude Code Reconciliation Directive

Karena source literal G2 belum ditemukan, Claude Code WAJIB melakukan reconciliation terhadap canonical corpus sebelum G2 dianggap fully canonical.

Claude Code harus mencari:

1\. PD-03 Governance Baseline;

2\. Platform Organization Specification;

3\. Volume 0 Platform Organization Master Map;

4\. Architecture Baseline;

5\. PD-02 interface definitions;

6\. PD-03 A1–A10;

7\. PD-03 B1–B10;

8\. PD-03 C1–C10;

9\. PD-03 D1–D10;

10\. PD-03 E1–E10;

11\. PD-03 F1–F10;

12\. existing Interface Registry;

13\. existing Interface Contracts;

14\. Platform Dependency Map;

15\. cross-platform ADR;

16\. canonical integration artifacts.

Claude Code harus mencari apakah source memiliki:

• canonical interface names;

• interface owners;

• interface consumers;

• interface providers;

• interface direction;

• interface purpose;

• interface lifecycle;

• interface contract;

• interface evidence;

• interface registry;

• interface authority;

• interface success criteria.

Jika ditemukan, source tersebut menggantikan bounded synthesis pada bagian terkait.

Jika tidak ditemukan:

Do not invent historical baseline.

Catat gap sebagai:

Architecture Gap — Claude Code Completion Required

⸻

57\. Success Criteria

G2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1\. seluruh material PD-03 cross-platform relationships memiliki interface boundary;

2\. interface purpose dapat dijelaskan;

3\. interface tidak menciptakan ownership transfer;

4\. interface tidak menciptakan hierarchy;

5\. interface tidak menciptakan authority baru;

6\. domain ownership masing-masing Platform tetap jelas;

7\. governance interface dapat dibedakan dari execution;

8\. compliance interface dapat dibedakan dari remediation ownership;

9\. certification interface dapat dibedakan dari implementation;

10\. Architecture interface tetap menghormati PD-02 authority;

11\. Security interface tetap menghormati PD-08 ownership;

12\. Quality interface tetap menghormati PD-09 ownership;

13\. interface dapat ditelusuri ke applicable authority;

14\. material interface dapat menghasilkan evidence;

15\. interface change dapat mengikuti applicable lifecycle;

16\. interface conflict memiliki escalation path;

17\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G3;

18\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G8;

19\. G2 tidak mengambil alih G9;

59\. Canonical Interface Principle

G2 — Platform Interface Architecture defines the explicit cross-platform interface relationships through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance interacts with other AIOS Platform Divisions. Interfaces shall preserve domain ownership, authority, accountability, and execution boundaries; support legitimate governance, compliance, evidence, policy applicability, control, and certification relationships; and shall not create organizational hierarchy or authority beyond the authority already established by the applicable architecture.

G3

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\# 1. Purpose

G3 menetapkan architectural boundary untuk dependency PD-03 terhadap Platform lain dalam AIOS.

G3 menjawab:

\> \*\*Platform apa yang menjadi dependency PD-03, mengapa dependency tersebut diperlukan, apa yang diberikan oleh dependency tersebut, dan bagaimana dependency tersebut tetap mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing Platform?\*\*

G3 merupakan child architecture dari:

\> \*\*G2 — Platform Interface Architecture\*\*

G2 menetapkan interface relationship.

G3 menetapkan dependency relationship yang berjalan melalui atau menggunakan interface tersebut.

\---

\# 2. Source Foundation

Baseline PD-03 menetapkan:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

├── Primary Dependencies

│ ├── Architecture

│ ├── Security

│ └── Quality

│

└── Governance Domain

Source secara eksplisit menetapkan:

Dependency ≠ Subordination

Architecture, Security, dan Quality merupakan dependencies, bukan subordinate organizational units.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

PD-03 juga dapat memiliki relationship dengan Platform lain dalam bentuk:

• governance dependency;

• compliance interface;

• evidence exchange;

• policy applicability;

• control relationship;

• certification relationship.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

3\. Scope Gate

3.1 In Scope

G3 mencakup:

• identification of cross-platform dependencies;

• dependency purpose;

• dependency direction;

• dependency boundary;

• dependency ownership;

• dependency inputs;

• dependency outputs;

• dependency evidence;

• dependency accountability;

• dependency impact;

• dependency failure;

• dependency escalation;

• dependency change;

• dependency retirement.

3.2 Out of Scope

G3 tidak menetapkan:

• organizational hierarchy;

• ownership transfer;

• detailed interface contract;

• technical API;

• runtime protocol;

• infrastructure configuration;

• security implementation;

• detailed service-level agreement;

• internal structure Platform lain.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Dependency Principle

Canonical principle:

A dependency enables one Platform to rely on another Platform's domain capability, information, evidence, authority, or output without transferring domain ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Dependency

≠

Ownership

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Dependency

≠

Command

Dependency

≠

Execution Ownership

Prinsip ini merupakan explicit organizational boundary PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Dependency vs Interface

G2:

Interface = architectural relationship mechanism.

G3:

Dependency = reliance relationship.

Model:

G2

Interface

↓

Relationship Mechanism

↓

G3

Dependency

↓

Reliance

Tidak setiap interface harus menjadi dependency.

Satu dependency dapat menggunakan satu atau lebih interface.

⸻

Dependency tidak memindahkan ownership.

PD-03

↓

Owns Security

PD-08 tetap memiliki domain ownership Security.

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan prinsip:

Each Platform Owns Its Domain.

12\. Dependency Classes

Berdasarkan source yang tersedia, dependency PD-03 dapat dibatasi ke beberapa class:

12.1 Governance Dependency

PD-03 membutuhkan domain lain untuk menjalankan governance responsibility.

12.2 Evidence Dependency

PD-03 membutuhkan evidence yang dimiliki atau dihasilkan domain lain.

12.3 Assessment Dependency

PD-03 membutuhkan domain expertise atau assessment dari Platform lain.

12.4 Operational Dependency

PD-03 membutuhkan operational information atau evidence dari execution domain.

12.5 Certification Dependency

PD-03 membutuhkan evidence atau participation dari subject Platform untuk certification.

Taxonomy ini adalah bounded synthesis, bukan taxonomy literal baseline.

PD-08

provides evidence to

PD-03

Direction tidak menentukan ownership.

0\. dependency purpose jelas;

0\. provider jelas;

0\. domain ownership jelas;

0\. dependency authority jelas;

0\. escalation path tersedia;

0\. dependency change dapat dikendalikan.

25\. Dependency Failure

Dependency failure dapat berupa:

• unavailable provider;

• missing evidence;

• stale information;

• invalid evidence;

• broken interface;

• unresolved ownership;

• authority conflict;

• delayed dependency;

• incompatible change;

• dependency retirement tanpa replacement.

Failure harus diperlakukan sebagai architectural/operational issue sesuai domain yang terkena dampak.

29\. Dependency Retirement

Retirement harus memastikan:

• outstanding obligations;

• evidence retention;

• replacement dependency;

• contract status;

• lifecycle consequences.

• authority conflict;

• critical evidence unavailable;

• compliance impact material;

• architecture impact material;

• security impact material;

• quality impact material.

Escalation harus diarahkan ke authority yang relevan.

51\. Canonical Dependency Principle

G3 — Cross-Platform Dependency Architecture defines the legitimate dependency relationships through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance relies on other AIOS Platform domains, while preserving explicit ownership, authority, accountability, and execution boundaries. Dependency relationships shall not create organizational subordination, transfer domain ownership, or create authority beyond the applicable governance mandate.

Dependency

≠

Ownership

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Dependency

≠

Command

Dependency

≠

Execution Ownership

51\. Canonical Dependency Principle

G3 — Cross-Platform Dependency Architecture defines the legitimate dependency relationships through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance relies on other AIOS Platform domains, while preserving explicit ownership, authority, accountability, and execution boundaries. Dependency relationships shall not create organizational subordination, transfer domain ownership, or create authority beyond the applicable governance mandate.

\## Freeze Decision

\*\*G5 — FROZEN, tetapi dengan \`SOURCE QUALIFICATION\`.\*\*

G6

\# Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Platform ID:\*\* PD-03

\> \*\*Official Name:\*\* Governance & Compliance

\> \*\*Volume:\*\* Volume 3

\> \*\*Part:\*\* Part G — Platform Integration

\> \*\*Section:\*\* G6 — Certification Integration Model

\> \*\*Predecessor:\*\* G5 — \[Source-Defined Title Required\]

\> \*\*Status:\*\* FROZEN

\> \*\*Gold Standard Review:\*\* PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

\---

\# 1. Purpose

G6 menetapkan model integrasi certification PD-03 dengan Platform lain dalam konteks:

\- certification governance;

\- certification scope;

\- applicable criteria;

\- certification evidence;

\- assessor/reviewer;

\- certification authority;

\- certification result;

\- certification validity;

\- certification review;

\- certification renewal.

G6 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana certification governance PD-03 berinteraksi dengan Platform yang menjadi subject certification tanpa mengambil alih domain execution atau implementation ownership Platform tersebut?\*\*

Certification diperlakukan sebagai \*\*governance mechanism\*\*, bukan sebagai mechanism untuk mengambil alih ownership.

\---

\# 2. Source Foundation

Baseline PD-03 menetapkan Certification sebagai salah satu primary responsibilities PD-03.

Cross-platform relationship PD-03 dapat mencakup:

\- governance dependency;

\- compliance interface;

\- evidence exchange;

\- policy applicability;

\- control relationship;

\- certification relationship. Performance Architecture Review.txt

Source juga menyatakan bahwa PD-03 dapat bergantung pada Platform lain untuk:

\- certification evidence;

\- technical context;

\- architecture assessment;

\- security evidence;

\- quality evidence;

\- operational evidence. Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian certification integration merupakan legitimate cross-platform concern dalam boundary PD-03.

\---

\# 3. Fundamental Certification Principle

\> \*\*Certification does not transfer implementation ownership.\*\*

Model:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Certification Governance

│

│ Certification Relationship

▼

Subject Platform

│

│ Domain Execution

▼

Domain Outcome

│

│ Evidence

▼

PD-03

Certification Assessment

Subject Platform tetap memiliki domain dan implementation responsibility-nya.

Source secara eksplisit menetapkan:

Certification ≠ Implementation.

⸻

G6 mencakup:

• certification relationship;

• certification scope;

• certification evidence;

• assessor/reviewer relationship;

• certification authority;

• certification result;

• certification validity;

• review condition;

• renewal condition;

• cross-platform evidence exchange;

• certification status;

• certification traceability.

4.2 Out of Scope

G6 tidak menetapkan:

• technical implementation;

• operational execution;

• infrastructure ownership;

• security implementation ownership;

• quality implementation ownership;

• architecture ownership;

• staffing;

• organizational hierarchy;

• technical design;

• undocumented certification standard;

• undocumented certification schema.

6\. Certification Authority

Certification authority harus memiliki:

• identifiable authority basis;

• defined certification scope;

• applicable criteria;

• authorized decision mechanism.

Authority tidak boleh muncul hanya karena suatu organizational function melakukan governance activity.

Certification authority harus dapat ditelusuri kepada applicable governance authority.

Source menetapkan bahwa certification harus memiliki authority sebagai salah satu elemen fundamental.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

• applicable system/domain;

• organizational or operational subject;

hanya apabila applicable certification criteria mengizinkannya.

G6 tidak mengasumsikan semua Platform harus selalu menjadi subject certification.

Subject harus ditetapkan oleh applicable certification scope.

Applicable Criteria

45\. Certification Boundary Rules

Rule 01 — Certification ≠ Implementation

Certification tidak memindahkan implementation ownership.

Rule 02 — Evidence ≠ Ownership Transfer

Evidence exchange tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

Rule 03 — Authority Must Be Explicit

Certification decision harus memiliki authority basis.

Rule 04 — Scope Must Be Explicit

Tidak ada certification tanpa defined scope.

Rule 05 — Criteria Must Be Applicable

Certification harus menggunakan applicable criteria.

Rule 06 — Result Must Be Traceable

Certification result harus dapat ditelusuri ke evidence dan assessment.

Rule 07 — Validity Must Be Explicit

Certification harus memiliki validity mechanism.

Rule 08 — Review Must Be Governed

Review/renewal harus mengikuti applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

kecuali canonical source menetapkannya.

6\. Certification Authority

Certification authority harus memiliki:

• identifiable authority basis;

• defined certification scope;

• applicable criteria;

• authorized decision mechanism.

Authority tidak boleh muncul hanya karena suatu organizational function melakukan governance activity.

Certification authority harus dapat ditelusuri kepada applicable governance authority.

Source menetapkan bahwa certification harus memiliki authority sebagai salah satu elemen fundamental.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

7\. Certification Scope

Setiap certification harus memiliki scope yang eksplisit.

Minimum scope identity:

Element Requirement

Subject Apa yang disertifikasi

Boundary Batas subject

Criteria Kriteria yang digunakan

Applicability Mengapa criteria berlaku

Evidence Bukti yang diperlukan

Assessor/Reviewer Pihak yang melakukan assessment

Authority Authority yang mengesahkan result

Result Outcome certification

Validity Masa berlaku

Renewal Kondisi review/renewal

Source secara eksplisit mendukung certification scope dan applicable criteria.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

8\. Certification Subject

Certification subject dapat berupa:

• Platform;

• capability;

• governance-controlled scope;

• applicable system/domain;

• organizational or operational subject;

hanya apabila applicable certification criteria mengizinkannya.

G6 tidak mengasumsikan semua Platform harus selalu menjadi subject certification.

Subject harus ditetapkan oleh applicable certification scope.

45\. Certification Boundary Rules

Rule 01 — Certification ≠ Implementation

Certification tidak memindahkan implementation ownership.

Rule 02 — Evidence ≠ Ownership Transfer

Evidence exchange tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

Rule 03 — Authority Must Be Explicit

Certification decision harus memiliki authority basis.

Rule 04 — Scope Must Be Explicit

Tidak ada certification tanpa defined scope.

Rule 05 — Criteria Must Be Applicable

Certification harus menggunakan applicable criteria.

Rule 06 — Result Must Be Traceable

Certification result harus dapat ditelusuri ke evidence dan assessment.

Rule 07 — Validity Must Be Explicit

Certification harus memiliki validity mechanism.

Rule 08 — Review Must Be Governed

Review/renewal harus mengikuti applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

kecuali canonical source menetapkannya.

\# 1. Purpose

G7 menetapkan architectural model untuk shared governance services

yang dapat digunakan oleh lebih dari satu Platform Division tanpa

memindahkan domain ownership dari Platform pengguna maupun dari

PD-03.

G7 menjawab:

\> Bagaimana governance capability yang bersifat reusable dapat

\> disediakan secara lintas Platform melalui hubungan resmi, tanpa

\> menciptakan organizational hierarchy atau mengambil alih execution

\> domain Platform lain?

G7 berada pada boundary:

\`\`\`text

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

│

│ Shared Governance Service

▼

Multiple Platform Divisions

Shared governance service merupakan mekanisme reuse dan integration, bukan mekanisme ownership transfer.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization menetapkan Part G — Platform Integration mencakup:

• Platform Interfaces;

• Cross-Platform Dependencies;

• Shared Services;

• Integration Contracts.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

PD-03 memiliki primary responsibility terhadap:

• Policy;

• Control;

• Certification;

serta memiliki Governance Standards sebagai primary output.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan Governance & Compliance sebagai primary organizational domain PD-03.

Dengan demikian shared governance service merupakan legitimate integration concern bagi PD-03 selama tetap berada dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

⸻

Source yang tersedia mendukung:

Part G

↓

PD-03

↓

Governance & Compliance

├── Policy

├── Control

├── Certification

└── Governance Standards

dan tidak memberikan canonical catalog yang menyatakan:

Substantive service catalog tetap membutuhkan reconciliation terhadap canonical source oleh Claude Code.

⸻

4\. Definition

Shared Governance Service adalah governance capability atau governance mechanism yang:

0\. berada dalam authorized Governance & Compliance scope;

0\. dapat digunakan oleh lebih dari satu Platform;

0\. memiliki definisi dan ownership yang jelas;

0\. memiliki interface atau interaction mechanism yang jelas;

0\. mempertahankan domain ownership Platform pengguna;

0\. tidak menciptakan organizational hierarchy;

0\. dapat digunakan secara reusable;

0\. dapat ditelusuri kepada governance authority.

Model:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

Shared Governance Service

│

┌────────────┼────────────┐

▼ ▼ ▼

PD-02 PD-08 PD-09

Architecture Security Quality

Diagram tersebut menunjukkan service relationship.

Diagram tersebut tidak menunjukkan organizational subordination.

⸻

Shared Governance Service enables reuse; it does not transfer ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Shared Service

≠

Shared Ownership

dan:

Service Consumer

≠

Subordinate Organization

61\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G7 — Shared Governance Services establishes the architectural model for reusable Governance & Compliance services that may be consumed across multiple AIOS Platform Divisions while preserving one primary service owner, explicit governance authority, consumer-domain ownership, Platform autonomy, evidence traceability, and the separation between governance responsibility and operational or technical execution.

G7 does not establish an unsupported technical service catalog, contract schema, SLA, or implementation topology. Such details remain subject to canonical source reconciliation.

Status: FROZEN — SOURCE-SUPPORTED, RECONCILIATION REQUIRED.

\# 1. Purpose

G7 menetapkan architectural model untuk shared governance services

yang dapat digunakan oleh lebih dari satu Platform Division tanpa

memindahkan domain ownership dari Platform pengguna maupun dari

PD-03.

G7 menjawab:

\> Bagaimana governance capability yang bersifat reusable dapat

\> disediakan secara lintas Platform melalui hubungan resmi, tanpa

\> menciptakan organizational hierarchy atau mengambil alih execution

\> domain Platform lain?

G7 berada pada boundary:

\`\`\`text

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

│

│ Shared Governance Service

▼

Multiple Platform Divisions

Shared governance service merupakan mekanisme reuse dan integration, bukan mekanisme ownership transfer.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization menetapkan Part G — Platform Integration mencakup:

• Platform Interfaces;

• Cross-Platform Dependencies;

• Shared Services;

• Integration Contracts.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

PD-03 memiliki primary responsibility terhadap:

• Policy;

• Control;

• Certification;

serta memiliki Governance Standards sebagai primary output.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Baseline juga menetapkan Governance & Compliance sebagai primary organizational domain PD-03.

Dengan demikian shared governance service merupakan legitimate integration concern bagi PD-03 selama tetap berada dalam domain Governance & Compliance.

⸻

4\. Definition

Shared Governance Service adalah governance capability atau governance mechanism yang:

1\. berada dalam authorized Governance & Compliance scope;

2\. dapat digunakan oleh lebih dari satu Platform;

3\. memiliki definisi dan ownership yang jelas;

4\. memiliki interface atau interaction mechanism yang jelas;

5\. mempertahankan domain ownership Platform pengguna;

6\. tidak menciptakan organizational hierarchy;

7\. dapat digunakan secara reusable;

8\. dapat ditelusuri kepada governance authority.

Model:

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

Shared Governance Service

│

┌────────────┼────────────┐

▼ ▼ ▼

PD-02 PD-08 PD-09

Architecture Security Quality

Diagram tersebut menunjukkan service relationship.

Diagram tersebut tidak menunjukkan organizational subordination.

⸻

Shared Governance Service enables reuse; it does not transfer ownership.

≠

dan:

≠

Subordinate Organization

61\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G7 — Shared Governance Services establishes the architectural model for reusable Governance & Compliance services that may be consumed across multiple AIOS Platform Divisions while preserving one primary service owner, explicit governance authority, consumer-domain ownership, Platform autonomy, evidence traceability, and the separation between governance responsibility and operational or technical execution.

G7 does not establish an unsupported technical service catalog, contract schema, SLA, or implementation topology. Such details remain subject to canonical source reconciliation.

Status: FROZEN — SOURCE-SUPPORTED, RECONCILIATION REQUIRED.

\# 1. Purpose

G8 menetapkan architectural foundation untuk \*\*Integration Contracts\*\*

yang mengatur hubungan integrasi antara PD-03 Governance & Compliance

dengan Platform atau domain lain.

G8 menjawab:

\> Bagaimana hubungan integrasi governance antar-Platform

\> didefinisikan secara eksplisit, dapat ditelusuri, memiliki

\> responsibility yang jelas, dan dapat dikendalikan sepanjang

\> lifecycle-nya?

Integration Contract berfungsi sebagai boundary mechanism antara:

\`\`\`text

PD-03

Governance & Compliance

│

│ Integration Contract

▼

Applicable Platform / Domain

G8 tidak mendefinisikan technical implementation contract apabila canonical source belum menetapkannya.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline Platform Organization menetapkan Part G — Platform Integration mencakup:

• Platform Interfaces;

• Cross-Platform Dependencies;

• Shared Services;

• Integration Contracts.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian:

Integration Contracts merupakan concern resmi Platform Integration Architecture.

PD-03 memiliki domain:

Governance & Compliance

dengan responsibility utama:

• Policy;

• Control;

• Certification;

serta Governance Standards sebagai primary output.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Karena itu Integration Contracts G8 harus dibatasi pada contract relationship yang berkaitan dengan Governance & Compliance.

4\. Definition

Integration Contract adalah explicit agreement boundary yang mendefinisikan bagaimana dua atau lebih Platform atau domain berinteraksi dalam suatu integration relationship.

Secara konseptual:

Provider / Source Domain

│

│ Integration Contract

▼

Consumer / Receiving Domain

Contract harus menjelaskan relationship yang diperlukan agar integrasi dapat dijalankan secara predictable dan accountable.

⸻

5\. Fundamental Principle

An integration contract defines a relationship; it does not transfer domain ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Integration Contract

≠

Ownership Transfer

dan:

Integration Contract

≠

Organizational Subordination

⸻

G8 mencakup:

• integration contract purpose;

• contract authority;

• contract lifecycle;

68\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G8 — Integration Contracts establishes the architectural foundation for explicit, governed, traceable, and accountable integration relationships between PD-03 and applicable Platforms or domains, while preserving domain ownership, Platform autonomy, governance authority, evidence traceability, and the separation between governance responsibility and technical or operational execution.

G8 does not establish an unsupported API, protocol, schema, SLA, SLO, or technical implementation model. Such details remain subject to canonical source reconciliation.

STATUS: FROZEN — SOURCE-SUPPORTED, RECONCILIATION REQUIRED.

yang mengatur hubungan integrasi antara PD-03 Governance & Compliance

dengan Platform atau domain lain.

G8 menjawab:

\> Bagaimana hubungan integrasi governance antar-Platform

\> didefinisikan secara eksplisit, dapat ditelusuri, memiliki

\> responsibility yang jelas, dan dapat dikendalikan sepanjang

\> lifecycle-nya?

Governance & Compliance

│ Integration Contract

▼

Applicable Platform / Domain

4\. Definition

Integration Contract adalah explicit agreement boundary yang mendefinisikan bagaimana dua atau lebih Platform atau domain berinteraksi dalam suatu integration relationship.

Secara konseptual:

Provider / Source Domain

│

│ Integration Contract

▼

Consumer / Receiving Domain

Contract harus menjelaskan relationship yang diperlukan agar integrasi dapat dijalankan secara predictable dan accountable.

⸻

5\. Fundamental Principle

An integration contract defines a relationship; it does not transfer domain ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Integration Contract

≠

Ownership Transfer

dan:

Integration Contract

≠

Organizational Subordination

68\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G8 — Integration Contracts establishes the architectural foundation for explicit, governed, traceable, and accountable integration relationships between PD-03 and applicable Platforms or domains, while preserving domain ownership, Platform autonomy, governance authority, evidence traceability, and the separation between governance responsibility and technical or operational execution.

G8 does not establish an unsupported API, protocol, schema, SLA, SLO, or technical implementation model. Such details remain subject to canonical source reconciliation.

STATUS: FROZEN — SOURCE-SUPPORTED, RECONCILIATION REQUIRED.

\> ownership domain Platform lain?\*\*

49\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G9 — Platform Integration Governance establishes the bounded governance foundation for managing material integration relationships within PD-03, including authority, accountability, review, compliance, evidence, traceability, escalation, and change governance, while preserving Platform ownership, Platform autonomy, architecture authority, and the separation between governance and execution.

Because the canonical source does not currently provide an explicit G9 identity or definition, this section is frozen only as a bounded structural synthesis. Claude Code must reconcile the canonical corpus before treating the G9 identity as fully canonical.

No unsupported technical integration mechanism, SLA, protocol, schema, or implementation detail is frozen by this section.

STATUS: FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION — CLAUDE CODE RECONCILIATION REQUIRED.

10\. Integration Success Dimension IS-01 — Governance Effectiveness

• governance requirement jelas;

• governance mechanism berjalan;

• decision path tersedia;

• review dapat dilakukan;

• escalation tersedia;

• outcome dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

11\. Integration Success Dimension IS-02 — Ownership Clarity

Integration dianggap berhasil apabila:

• provider jelas;

• consumer jelas;

• domain owner jelas;

• governance owner jelas;

• accountability jelas.

Prinsip:

Collaboration

≠

Multiple Primary Owners

Setiap responsibility tetap memiliki primary owner.

⸻

12\. Integration Success Dimension IS-03 — Interface Integrity

Interface governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• interface memiliki ownership;

• interface relationship dapat diidentifikasi;

• applicable requirements diketahui;

• perubahan dapat dikendalikan;

• material interface issue dapat direview.

G10 tidak menetapkan technical interface implementation.

⸻

13\. Integration Success Dimension IS-04 — Dependency Integrity

Dependency governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• dependency dapat diidentifikasi;

• dependency owner jelas;

• dependency impact dapat dinilai;

• dependency change dapat dikendalikan;

• dependency failure memiliki response path.

Dependency tidak berarti ownership transfer.

⸻

14\. Integration Success Dimension IS-05 — Contract Integrity

Integration contract governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• contract relationship jelas;

• parties dapat diidentifikasi;

• obligations dapat ditelusuri;

• authority tersedia;

• material changes dikendalikan;

• breach dapat diidentifikasi dan dieskalasikan.

G10 tidak mendefinisikan technical contract schema.

⸻

15\. Integration Success Dimension IS-06 — Certification Integrity

Certification integration dianggap berhasil apabila:

• certification relationship dapat ditelusuri;

• applicable requirement jelas;

• evidence tersedia;

• assessment dapat dilakukan;

• finding memiliki remediation path;

• certification impact dapat dieskalasikan.

G10 tidak menggantikan Certification Integration Model pada G6.

Assessment

↓

Corrective Action

Decision

18\. Integration Success Dimension IS-09 — Review Effectiveness

Integration review dianggap efektif apabila:

• material integration matter dapat direview;

• review menggunakan applicable authority;

• evidence digunakan;

• findings dicatat;

• action ownership jelas;

• closure dapat diverifikasi.

Review tidak boleh menjadi substitute untuk domain execution.

⸻

19\. Integration Success Dimension IS-10 — Escalation Effectiveness

Escalation dianggap efektif apabila:

• conflict dapat diidentifikasi;

• applicable authority dapat ditentukan;

• evidence tersedia;

• local resolution dicoba jika appropriate;

• escalation dilakukan ketika required;

• outcome dicatat.

Identify Conflict

↓

Classify Domain

↓

Identify Applicable Authority

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve Locally if Possible

↓

Escalate if Required

↓

Record Outcome

• authority decision tersedia;

• implementation dapat ditelusuri;

• documentation diperbarui;

• verification dilakukan.

25\. Success Failure Conditions

Integration success dapat dianggap gagal atau incomplete apabila:

• ownership ambigu;

• authority tidak jelas;

• material relationship tidak governed;

• dependency tidak dapat ditelusuri;

• contract tidak dapat diverifikasi;

• evidence material tidak tersedia;

• compliance finding tidak ditindaklanjuti;

• material change bypass governance;

• conflict tidak memiliki resolution path;

• escalation tidak dapat mencapai applicable authority;

• integration governance menghilangkan Platform autonomy.

Improvement Review

\# 1. Purpose

G10 menetapkan bounded success model untuk menilai apakah

Platform Integration dalam konteks PD-03 Governance & Compliance

telah menghasilkan hubungan lintas-Platform yang:

\- governed;

\- accountable;

\- traceable;

\- compliant;

\- reviewable;

\- controlled;

\- sustainable;

\- aligned dengan domain ownership.

G10 menjadi terminal section Part G.

5\. Fundamental Success Principle

Integration success is not the absence of integration problems; it is the presence of controlled, accountable, traceable, and sustainable integration relationships.

Dengan demikian:

Integration Exists

≠

Integration Success

dan:

Integration Success

\=

Governed Relationship

-

Clear Ownership

-

Traceability

-

Compliance

-

Effective Control

-

Sustainable Collaboration

⸻

6.1 In Scope

G10 mencakup penilaian terhadap:

• integration governance effectiveness;

• certification integration;

• cross-platform accountability;

• compliance;

6.2 Out of Scope

7\. Platform Ownership Principle

Integration success tidak boleh mengubah domain ownership.

Canonical principle:

Governance responsibility does not automatically transfer domain ownership.

Model:

9\. Success Model

Bounded G10 Success Model:

Integration Structure

↓

Integration Governance

↓

Integration Accountability

↓

Integration Compliance

↓

Integration Traceability

↓

Integration Effectiveness

↓

Integration Improvement

↓

Integration Sustainability

Success hanya dapat dianggap tercapai apabila seluruh dimensi material yang applicable dapat dibuktikan.

⸻

10\. Integration Success Dimension IS-01 — Governance Effectiveness

Integration governance dianggap efektif apabila:

• governance requirement jelas;

• authority dapat diidentifikasi;

• governance mechanism berjalan;

• decision path tersedia;

• review dapat dilakukan;

• escalation tersedia;

• outcome dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

11\. Integration Success Dimension IS-02 — Ownership Clarity

Integration dianggap berhasil apabila:

• provider jelas;

• consumer jelas;

• domain owner jelas;

• governance owner jelas;

• accountability jelas.

Prinsip:

Collaboration

≠

Multiple Primary Owners

Setiap responsibility tetap memiliki primary owner.

⸻

12\. Integration Success Dimension IS-03 — Interface Integrity

Interface governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• interface memiliki ownership;

• interface relationship dapat diidentifikasi;

• applicable requirements diketahui;

• perubahan dapat dikendalikan;

• material interface issue dapat direview.

G10 tidak menetapkan technical interface implementation.

⸻

13\. Integration Success Dimension IS-04 — Dependency Integrity

Dependency governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• dependency dapat diidentifikasi;

• dependency owner jelas;

• dependency impact dapat dinilai;

• dependency change dapat dikendalikan;

• dependency failure memiliki response path.

Dependency tidak berarti ownership transfer.

⸻

14\. Integration Success Dimension IS-05 — Contract Integrity

Integration contract governance dianggap berhasil apabila:

• contract relationship jelas;

• parties dapat diidentifikasi;

• obligations dapat ditelusuri;

• authority tersedia;

• material changes dikendalikan;

• breach dapat diidentifikasi dan dieskalasikan.

G10 tidak mendefinisikan technical contract schema.

⸻

15\. Integration Success Dimension IS-06 — Certification Integrity

Certification integration dianggap berhasil apabila:

• certification relationship dapat ditelusuri;

• applicable requirement jelas;

• evidence tersedia;

• assessment dapat dilakukan;

• finding memiliki remediation path;

• certification impact dapat dieskalasikan.

G10 tidak menggantikan Certification Integration Model pada G6.

⸻

16\. Integration Success Dimension IS-07 — Compliance Integrity

Compliance dianggap berhasil apabila:

Requirement

↓

Applicable Integration Relationship

↓

Execution

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Finding / Outcome

↓

Corrective Action

↓

Verification

Compliance assessment harus memiliki traceability.

⸻

17\. Integration Success Dimension IS-08 — Traceability

Material integration matter harus dapat ditelusuri:

Requirement

↓

Interface / Dependency / Contract / Service

↓

Owner

↓

Governance Action

↓

Decision

↓

Evidence

↓

Outcome

Traceability menjadi indikator governance integrity.

⸻

18\. Integration Success Dimension IS-09 — Review Effectiveness

Integration review dianggap efektif apabila:

• material integration matter dapat direview;

• review menggunakan applicable authority;

• evidence digunakan;

• findings dicatat;

• action ownership jelas;

• closure dapat diverifikasi.

Review tidak boleh menjadi substitute untuk domain execution.

⸻

19\. Integration Success Dimension IS-10 — Escalation Effectiveness

Escalation dianggap efektif apabila:

• conflict dapat diidentifikasi;

• applicable authority dapat ditentukan;

• evidence tersedia;

• local resolution dicoba jika appropriate;

• escalation dilakukan ketika required;

• outcome dicatat.

Canonical conflict path:

Identify Conflict

↓

Classify Domain

↓

Identify Applicable Authority

↓

Review Evidence

↓

Resolve Locally if Possible

↓

Escalate if Required

↓

Record Outcome

Model tersebut selaras dengan Governance Conflict Model.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

20\. Integration Success Dimension IS-11 — Change Governance

Integration change dianggap berhasil apabila:

• material change dapat diidentifikasi;

• impact dapat dinilai;

• affected Platform diketahui;

• authority decision tersedia;

• implementation dapat ditelusuri;

• documentation diperbarui;

• verification dilakukan.

⸻

21\. Integration Success Dimension IS-12 — Improvement

Integration governance tidak dianggap mature apabila hanya mampu mempertahankan kondisi existing.

Success harus menyediakan improvement loop:

Review

↓

Finding

↓

Root Cause

↓

Improvement Opportunity

↓

Change

↓

Validation

↓

Learning

⸻

22\. Integration Success Dimension IS-13 — Sustainability

Integration relationship dianggap sustainable apabila:

• ownership tetap jelas;

• governance mechanism tetap dapat digunakan;

• dependency dapat dikelola;

• contract tetap traceable;

• evidence tetap tersedia;

• changes dapat dikendalikan;

• relationship dapat direview kembali.

Sustainability tidak berarti integration harus permanent.

⸻

23\. Integration Success Assessment

Assessment model:

Assessment

↓

Evidence Review

↓

Governance Evaluation

↓

Finding Identification

↓

Success Determination

↓

Improvement Recommendation

G10 tidak menciptakan independent enterprise performance framework.

⸻

24\. Evidence Requirement

Material success claim harus memiliki evidence apabila evidence dibutuhkan oleh applicable governance requirement.

Potential evidence:

• integration contract;

• interface record;

• dependency record;

• governance decision;

• certification record;

• compliance assessment;

• review record;

• change record;

• corrective action;

• closure evidence.

Retention duration tidak dibekukan karena canonical retention requirement G10 tidak ditemukan.

⸻

25\. Success Failure Conditions

Integration success dapat dianggap gagal atau incomplete apabila:

• ownership ambigu;

• authority tidak jelas;

• material relationship tidak governed;

• dependency tidak dapat ditelusuri;

• contract tidak dapat diverifikasi;

• evidence material tidak tersedia;

• compliance finding tidak ditindaklanjuti;

• material change bypass governance;

• conflict tidak memiliki resolution path;

• escalation tidak dapat mencapai applicable authority;

• integration governance menghilangkan Platform autonomy.

⸻

26\. Integration Success vs Platform Success

G10 hanya menilai integration success.

G10 tidak mengambil alih success criteria Platform lain.

Prinsip:

Platform Success

\=

Domain-Owned

Integration Success

\=

Relationship-Governed

Dengan demikian:

PD-03

Owns Governance & Compliance

Other Platform

Owns Its Domain Outcome

Governance success dan domain success harus tetap dibedakan.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

⸻

27\. Integration Success vs Performance

Integration success bukan performance measurement framework.

Integration Success

≠

Technical Performance

Performance architecture dapat menggunakan integration evidence sebagai input apabila applicable.

Namun performance ownership tetap berada pada applicable Performance Architecture.

⸻

28\. Integration Success vs Compliance

Compliance merupakan salah satu dimension of success.

Namun:

Compliance

≠

Entire Integration Success

Integration dapat compliant tetapi ineffective.

Sebaliknya, relationship yang operationally useful tetapi tidak governed belum dapat dinyatakan successful.

⸻

29\. Integration Success vs Governance

Governance menyediakan control mechanism.

G10 menilai outcome dari governance terhadap integration.

G9

Integration Governance

↓

G10

Integration Success

G10 tidak membuat governance architecture baru.

⸻

30\. Integration Success vs Lifecycle

G10 harus tetap aligned dengan Part F.

Integration success harus dapat dievaluasi sepanjang lifecycle:

Initiate

↓

Design

↓

Approve

↓

Activate

↓

Operate

↓

Review

↓

Change

↓

Improve

↓

Retire

Success bukan hanya kondisi pada saat initial activation.

⸻

31\. Integration Success Review Rhythm

Karena canonical G10 review rhythm tidak ditemukan, bounded model berikut digunakan sebagai candidate:

Periodic Review

Evaluasi berkala terhadap material integration relationship.

Event-Based Review

Evaluasi setelah:

• major integration change;

• major governance issue;

• certification event;

• material dependency change;

• architecture evolution.

Improvement Review

Evaluasi setelah:

• material finding;

• corrective action;

• repeated integration failure;

• governance learning.

Review rhythm harus direkonsiliasi terhadap Part D dan lifecycle baseline.

⸻

• Integration Success Assessment;

Formal reporting schema tidak dibekukan karena canonical source belum ditemukan.

34\. Cross-Platform Success

Cross-platform integration dianggap successful apabila:

• dependency dapat dikelola;

• interfaces dapat dikelola;

• contracts dapat dipertanggungjawabkan;

• shared services dapat digunakan sesuai governance;

• conflicts memiliki resolution path;

• domain ownership tetap jelas;

• governance compliance dapat dibuktikan.

44\. Canonical Freeze Statement

G10 — Platform Integration Success establishes the bounded success model for determining whether material Platform Integration relationships within PD-03 are governed, accountable, traceable, compliant, reviewable, controllable, and sustainable while preserving Platform ownership and Platform autonomy.

G10 does not create a new technical performance framework, technical integration KPI, SLA, SLO, protocol, implementation standard, or maturity scoring model.

Because the canonical source does not currently provide an explicit G10 identity or definition, this section is frozen as a bounded structural synthesis and must be reconciled by Claude Code before being treated as fully canonical.

⸻
